# Graph Report - Macro_Copilot  (2026-05-22)

## Corpus Check
- 661 files · ~1,201,530 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 15722 nodes · 30936 edges · 971 communities (732 shown, 239 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 75% EXTRACTED · 25% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 7673 edges (avg confidence: 0.72)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `de3171ed`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
